### `CONTINUE`

The task is detached from the RPC that created it and remains active.

**Type:** `BotTaskDisconnectPolicy.CONTINUE`.

**Value:** `1`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/task_pb.ts#L2944)

This policy is rejected by the unary StartBotTask RPC.

**Type:** `BotTaskDisconnectPolicy.CANCEL_WITH_CALL`.

## ExploreTask

**Type** in `generated/soulfire/task_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/task_pb.ts#L1562).

Visits coordinated frontier cells around an origin.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/task_pb`.

```ts
type ExploreTask = Message<"soulfire.v1.ExploreTask"> & {
  /**
   * Omitted origin uses the bot position when the task starts.
   *
   * @generated from field: optional soulfire.v1.BlockPosition origin = 1;
   */
  origin?: BlockPosition | undefined;

  /**
   * Maximum horizontal distance from the origin. Zero selects the server
   * default.
   *
   * @generated from field: uint32 radius = 2;
   */
  radius: number;

  /**
   * Distance between frontier cells. Zero selects the server default.
   *
   * @generated from field: uint32 waypoint_spacing = 3;
   */
  waypointSpacing: number;

  /**
   * Zero explores every frontier cell or runs until cancellation.
   *
   * @generated from field: uint32 maximum_waypoints = 4;
   */
  maximumWaypoints: number;

  /**
   * @generated from field: soulfire.v1.PathfindOptions options = 5;
   */
  options?: PathfindOptions | undefined;

  /**
   * Returns to the origin after a bounded waypoint limit.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool return_to_origin = 6;
   */
  returnToOrigin: boolean;

  /**
   * Shared purpose groups frontier claims across bots.
   *
   * @generated from field: string purpose = 7;
   */
  purpose: string;
};
```

## SleepTask

Finds, approaches, and enters a bed. A supplied position pins the task to
one bed; otherwise the nearest loaded bed is selected.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/task_pb`.

```ts
  /**
   * @generated from field: optional soulfire.v1.BlockPosition bed = 1;
   */
  bed?: BlockPosition | undefined;

  /**

   *
   * @generated from field: uint32 search_radius = 2;
   */
  searchRadius: number;

  /**
   * @generated from field: soulfire.v1.PathfindOptions options = 3;
   */
  options?: PathfindOptions | undefined;

  /**
   * Keeps retrying while sleep is unavailable, such as during daytime.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool wait_until_possible = 4;
   */
  waitUntilPossible: boolean;

  /**
   * Delay between rejected sleep attempts. Zero selects the server default.
   *
   * @generated from field: uint32 retry_interval_ticks = 5;
   */
  retryIntervalTicks: number;
};
```
